## Best date plans

### 2026-10-17 — Brooklyn Navy Yard Annual Open House + Haenyeo

**Brooklyn Navy Yard Annual Open House, then a meal.** Korean at Haenyeo. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Building+77%2C+141+Flushing+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=239+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/) · [Restaurant](https://www.haenyeobk.com/)

### 2026-10-15 — BUILD THE FUTURE public-art fence installation + The Four Horsemen

**BUILD THE FUTURE public-art fence installation, then a meal.** Wine bar and New American at The Four Horsemen. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Building+3+fence+line+between+Clermont+Ave+and+Adelphi+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=295+Grand+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/) · [Restaurant](https://www.fourhorsemenbk.com/)

### 2026-10-21 — Yellow Rose + Robert Glasper Experiment, Questlove, and Bilal

**Dinner, then Robert Glasper Experiment, Questlove, and Bilal.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is a 13-minute walk from the venue. Robert Glasper Experiment, Questlove, and Bilal starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=102+3rd+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=131+W.+Third+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10012&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://www.bluenote.net) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43377431-robert-glasper-experiment-at-blue-note-jazz-club) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosenyc.com/)

### 2026-10-21 — NYC Resistor Masks-On Craft Night + Gage & Tollner

**NYC Resistor Masks-On Craft Night, then dinner.** American steakhouse and seafood at Gage & Tollner. It is an 11-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=372+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-1st-fridays-and-3rd-wednesdays-of-the-month-masks-on-tickets-623419182557) · [Restaurant](https://www.gageandtollner.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.gageandtollner.com/reservations)

### 2026-10-21 — The Four Horsemen + Yaya Bey with H31R

**Dinner, then Yaya Bey with H31R.** Wine bar and New American at The Four Horsemen. It is an 8-minute walk from the venue. Yaya Bey with H31R starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=295+Grand+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=146+Broadway%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://babysallright.com) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43312544-yaya-bey-at-babys-all-right) · [Restaurant](https://www.fourhorsemenbk.com/)

### 2026-10-18 — Smorgasburg Prospect Park + Sofreh

**Smorgasburg Prospect Park, then a meal.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park+%28enter+at+Lincoln+Road%29%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=75+St+Marks+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Event booking](https://smorgasburg.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/reservations)

### 2026-10-16 — Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi + Mount Eerie with Mirah

**Dinner, then Mount Eerie with Mirah.** Afro-Caribbean and New York-inspired at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is a 4-minute walk from the venue. Mount Eerie with Mirah starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=2+W.+64th+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43345781-mount-eerie-at-new-york-society-for-ethical-culture) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-10-20 — Rule of Thirds + Amber Mark

**Dinner, then Amber Mark.** Japanese at Rule of Thirds. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Amber Mark starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+Banker+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11222&destination=131+West+Third+Street&travelmode=walking) · [Event](http://www.bluenote.net) · [Event booking](https://www.ticketweb.com/event/amber-mark-blue-note-jazz-club-tickets/15047273?REFERRAL_ID=tmfeedbuyat234705&wt.mc_id=aff_BUYAT_234705&camefrom=CFC_BUYAT_234705) · [Restaurant](https://www.ruleofthirdsnyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.ruleofthirdsnyc.com/reservations)

#### [Art in the Garden](https://www.thehort.org/event/art-in-the-garden-30/)

5:00 PM · workshop · Free · Greenhouse Education Center · Hamilton Heights · Manhattan · Score 70.0  
Free photo printing and collage workshop looking at garden archives and June Jordan in Harlem, facilitated by Stephanie Alvarado.
**Timing:** Date confirmed for October 15; exact time was not exposed in the fetched listing. Date re-verified against the venue: moved from 2026-10-15 to 2026-10-15.
**Practical note:** Greenhouse event happens rain or shine; venue notes pebbled/grass paths and accessibility accommodations with notice.
**Why now:** The Horticultural Society of New York lists this public workshop for Thursday, October 15.
**On-sale watch:** Free workshop capacity may be limited.

#### [BUILD THE FUTURE public-art fence installation](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/)

10:00 AM · outdoors · Free · Brooklyn Navy Yard · Brooklyn Navy Yard · Brooklyn · Score 68.0  
Peter Miller's 350-foot public-art installation uses woven colored aluminum strips to create a shifting three-dimensional message along the Yard fence line.
**Timing:** Ongoing outdoor installation through November 1.
**Practical note:** Outdoor public-art walk; NWS forecast is mostly sunny with a high near 64 on October 15.
**Why now:** Brooklyn Navy Yard lists the installation as running June 4-November 1, covering the window.
[Official](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/)

## Where to eat

Checked the candidates against captured official/public evidence and limited follow-up pages, keeping current, useful dining entries while correcting price, timing, classification, and duplicate issues.

Dining research: 2026-10-15T10:33:22+00:00

No Michelin-star claims were verified or retained. The Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin-related recognition for the Texas pitmasters’ prior restaurants/awards, not for Kirbee’s itself; no NYC Michelin star or Guide-selection claim was added. Several official restaurant pages exposed only shells/widgets, so captured HTML/PDF/JSON-LD evidence was used where clearer.

### Skyline lunch prix fixe: two or three courses

Special menus — Manhatta

A high-view, reservation-friendly lunch option with a clearly stated two- or three-course prix-fixe format; useful for a polished weekday or weekend midday meal without the dinner price.

Price: Two Course | 65; Three Courses | 78  
Schedule: Lunch 11:30AM-2:00PM daily  
Conditions: Reservations accepted up to 28 days in advance; walk-ins welcome. Tax, tip, and service charges not stated on checked page.  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/restaurant-menu/)

### Thanksgiving four-course prix fixe with skyline view

Special menus — Manhatta

A bounded holiday menu from Executive Chef Michele Brogioni, useful for a special-occasion meal with a live reservation link.

Price: $225 per person for the Thanksgiving four-course menu; official restaurant-menu page labels it “Four Courses | 225” and says the sample menu may vary.  
Schedule: Thanksgiving; reservation link prefilled for 2026-11-26 16:00  
Conditions: Official holiday page confirms a special Thanksgiving four-course prix fixe; official menu page supplies the $225 price and sample-menu caveat. Tax, tip, service charges, and booking terms not stated on checked pages. Sources: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/holidaysnyc/ and https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/restaurant-menu/  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/holidaysnyc/)

### Weekday seafood-leaning happy hour

Deals & happy hours — Hav & Mar

Marcus Samuelsson’s Chelsea restaurant has an officially stated weekday happy hour, useful before gallery/High Line plans; menu prices were not exposed by the checked Marqii menu widget.

Schedule: Monday-Friday 5:00 pm-7:00 pm  
Conditions: Happy-hour item prices and purchase minimums not stated on checked official pages; reservations available through site widget. Tax, tip, and service charges unknown.  
[Source](https://www.havandmar.com/location/hav-mar/)

### Back to Green cookbook-inspired fall/winter menu

Special menus — Hav & Mar

A chef/cookbook-linked special: after Marcus Samuelsson’s Back to Green launch at PAC NYC, Hav & Mar says it will serve a limited-edition menu inspired by the book throughout fall and winter.

Schedule: Back to Green launch conversation October 20 at 5:30 PM at PAC NYC/Metropolis; Hav & Mar says the limited-edition menu follows throughout fall and winter.  
Conditions: Specific restaurant menu start date, end date, dishes, price, tax, tip, and service charges were not stated. The October 20 ticketed event is at PAC NYC/Metropolis, not Hav & Mar. Source: https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/  
[Source](https://www.havandmar.com/happenings/)

### Crown Shy weekday lunch with a prix-fixe option noted

More restaurants — Crown Shy

A weekday FiDi lunch option from a serious kitchen; the official menu states a lunch prix-fixe option is available and lists current lunch dishes such as pork katsu sandwich, rigatoni, salmon, and chicken kofta.

Price: Prix-fixe price unknown; a la carte examples checked: appetizers $19-$25, entrees $27-$49  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 11:30am-2pm; dinner daily  
Conditions: Treat as a restaurant lunch entry, not a verified prix-fixe special: the official menu says “Prix Fixe Menu Available” but does not state the prix-fixe price, required courses, tax, tip, or service charges. Lunch hours are Monday-Friday 11:30am-2pm. Sources: https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/ and https://www.crownshy.nyc/location/crown-shy/  
[Source](https://www.crownshy.nyc/menus/)

### Good Time Country Buffet fixed-price Southern buffet

More restaurants — Good Time Country Buffet

A genuine September opening from the Kisa and C as in Charlie team, offering an all-you-can-eat Southern buffet with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rib tips, and rotating specials.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 ages 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5-10 p.m.  
Conditions: Eater reports the September 12 opening, buffet prices, Wednesday-Sunday 5-10 p.m. hours, walk-ins, and limited Resy reservations; the official site checked as only a minimal welcome page. Drinks and desserts cost extra; tax, tip, and service charges not stated. Sources: https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa and https://www.goodtimebuffet.com/  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
Published: 2026-09-11  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Kirbee’s daytime Greenpoint barbecue restaurant

More restaurants — Kirbee’s

A notable collaboration from Barbs B Q’s Chuck Charnichart and Goldee’s Jonny White, with plates split between South Texas/Mexican flavors and modern Texas barbecue: brisket, pork ribs, green spaghetti, turkey, potato salad, coleslaw, and puddings.

Schedule: Opening-day and Labor Day weekend details were reported by Eater; ongoing current hours were not verified on the official site.  
Conditions: The August 30 limited-service first day and Labor Day weekend opening window have passed for an October 15 guide. Keep as a recent restaurant entry, not an active opening event. Official site exposed only a cookie notice in this check; current hours, prices, tax, tip, and service charges remain unknown.
